# Skills · P · part 45

812 entries, pearson-correlation-of-count-matrices to peer-comparison. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pearson-correlation-of-count-matrices**: Use when two raw gene-barcode count matrices produced by different scRNA-seq pipelines on the same FASTQ dataset must be compared numerically to validate that an alternative pipeline reproduces native-pipeline output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-count-matrices))
- **pearson-correlation-of-cross-model-scores**: Use when two sequence-based variant-effect models (e.g. DeepFun and DeepSEA) have been run on the same SNP set and you must quantify per-assay score agreement, such as CTCF, DNase-seq, H3K4me3, or H3K9ac concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-cross-model-scores))
- **pearson-correlation-of-crup-probability-tracks**: Reference for computing or benchmarking the cor_CRUP generic feature (Pearson correlation between summed CRUP-EP and CRUP-PP probability tracks across cell types) when building or ablation-testing ET-interaction feature vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-crup-probability-tracks))
- **pearson-correlation-of-fold-change-signatures**: Use when two differential analysis results (e.g., young-vs-old log2 fold-change per ATAC-seq peak in men vs women) must be compared on a common feature set to measure how much of the response is shared versus sex- or condition-specific. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-fold-change-signatures))
- **pearson-correlation-of-genomic-tracks**: Use when you have CscoreTool-M cscore.txt or cscore.bedgraph sub-compartment probabilities and an independent track (NAD-seq, Lamin-B1 DamID) that can be aggregated over the same 100 kb windows, and you want to quantify agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-genomic-tracks))
- **pearson-correlation-of-pathway-activity-with-clinical-phenotype**: Use when you have a gene expression matrix paired with a continuous clinical variable (e.g. 1-hour OGTT blood glucose) and need to test which of ~400 predefined KEGG/Biocarta pathways' activity levels correlate with the phenotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-pathway-activity-with-clinical-phenotype))
- **pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas**: Use when validating a GC×GC–TOFMS peak-picking/quantification workflow against reference software using paired peak-area tables, especially with a serial dilution series (e.g. 1 ppm Breath Mix QC) where both correlation and dilution linearity must be shown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas))
- **pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas-to-mixing-ratios**: Use when evaluating metabolomics peak-area quantification accuracy on a dilution/mixing series with known expected ratios, especially comparing two processing tools' feature tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-peak-areas-to-mixing-ratios))
- **pearson-correlation-of-per-protein-background-levels**: Use when a CITE-seq (or ASAP-seq/TEA-seq) ADT dataset has both a raw empty-droplet matrix and a cell-containing matrix, and you need to verify that empty-droplet counts faithfully capture the ambient antibody background before using them for dsb step I correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-per-protein-background-levels))
- **pearson-correlation-of-per-site-methylation-levels**: Use when comparing methylation levels between a targeted/enrichment Nanopore run (e.g., RRMS adaptive sampling) and a whole-genome Nanopore run to quantify concordance of per-site CpG methylation frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-per-site-methylation-levels))
- **pearson-correlation-of-perturbation-ranking-profiles**: Use when comparing perturbation effect ranking lists produced under alternative MUSIC preprocessing settings (imputation on/off, Cell_filtering on/off) to measure ranking-list similarity and identify which strategy shifts the ranking most. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-perturbation-ranking-profiles))
- **pearson-correlation-of-umi-counts**: Use when two aligned gene-barcode matrices (same barcodes, zero-padded genes) from different processing pipelines must be quantitatively compared at the UMI-count level to assess processing concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-of-umi-counts))
- **pearson-correlation-outlier-cell-filtering**: Use when a scChIP-seq or scATAC-seq count matrix has passed read-count QC (e.g. ≥1600 unique reads, top 5% coverage removed) but may still contain isolated low-quality outlier cells before dimensionality reduction and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-outlier-cell-filtering))
- **pearson-correlation-permutation-significance-testing**: Use when building gene co-expression networks from RNA-Seq expression matrices where Pearson-correlation edges must be filtered for statistical significance before fusing with other similarity-based networks (e.g., Euclidean-distance networks). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-permutation-significance-testing))
- **pearson-correlation-protein-network-inference**: Use when you need a plain co-expression/PCC baseline network on a protein-by-condition matrix (tsv/csv, e.g. HPA 566,109-cell scRNA-seq or PRIDE bulk proteomics) to benchmark against a learned method like FAVA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-protein-network-inference))
- **pearson-correlation-replicate-reproducibility-analysis**: Use when you have expression quantification from ≥2 sequencing replicates (or two quantification tools on the same samples) and need to verify reproducibility (e.g., replicate Pearson r &gt; 0.971) or cross-tool consistency (e.g., r ≈ 0.83). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-replicate-reproducibility-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-sample-assignment**: Use when each sample in a log2-transformed, median-centered tumor expression matrix (e.g., the SRIQ '_mc_log2_nz' output) must be labeled with a nearest-centroid TRU/PI/PP call to compare against an independent clustering solution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-sample-assignment))
- **pearson-correlation-scatter-visualization**: Use when validating that clustering features (e.g., Spearman correlation features from sscClust) remain robust to subsampling fluctuations across multiple subsampling rates of an scRNA-seq dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-scatter-visualization))
- **pearson-correlation-significance-testing**: Use when you need to quantify and significance-test the association between two per-cell score vectors (e.g., AXL-high vs MITF-high program scores in scRNA-seq) and compare that association before vs after confounder correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-significance-testing))
- **pearson-correlation-significance-testing-by-random-shuffling**: Use when two co-registered LA-ICP-MS elemental images (identical pixel dimensions) require quantification of spatial colocalisation, and a significance probability for the observed Pearson R is needed beyond the raw correlation coefficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-significance-testing-by-random-shuffling))
- **pearson-correlation-similarity-assessment**: Use when validating that computationally inferred (semi-profiled) single-cell data reproduces the ground-truth cohort structure, by correlating per-condition cell-type proportion vectors and pathway activation scores between two cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-similarity-assessment))
- **pearson-correlation-spectral-similarity-assessment**: Use when you have generated a simulated 1D or 2D metabolomic 1H NMR spectrum (urine, blood, or CSF) and need an objective, scalar measure of its resemblance to a real experimental spectrum before using the simulation for algorithm development or validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-spectral-similarity-assessment))
- **pearson-correlation-stability-analysis**: Use when a model has a stochastic component (e.g. random GAN initialization) and you must test whether an internal loss reliably predicts external alignment quality across repeated runs, via per-run Pearson correlations between loss and metric trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-stability-analysis))
- **pearson-correlation-statistical-testing**: Use when when you have two co-registered LA-ICP-MS element images and need to determine whether the spatial distribution of one element correlates significantly with another. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-statistical-testing))
- **pearson-correlation-statistics**: Use when you have paired pairwise dissimilarity values (e.g., MSA-based MSD and phylogenetic branch length BLD) between OTU representative sequences and their member reads, and need to quantify agreement at read vs. OTU level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/pearson-correlation-statistics))
- **pearson-correlation-strategy-selection**: Decision support for choosing NeuCA's annotation route when a labelled training scRNA-seq matrix is available: compute cross-cell-type correlations and check whether any off-diagonal element reaches tau = 0.95. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-strategy-selection))
- **pearson-correlation-testing**: Use when interpreting discriminant MOFA (or DIABLO) factors by testing their association with clinical metadata — numeric features via Pearson correlation, binary features via Wilcoxon — inside the BiomiX R pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-testing))
- **pearson-correlation-testing-of-ion-pairs**: Use when an annotated massdiff object of matched adduct ion pairs has been generated from MSI peak data and the candidate parent–adduct pairs must be tested for spatial co-localization before being accepted as true adducts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-testing-of-ion-pairs))
- **pearson-correlation-validation**: Use when a newly proposed clustering evaluation metric must be validated against an established ground-truth evaluation on a dataset where reference results exist, via Pearson correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pearson-correlation-validation))
- **pearson-correlation-validation**: Use when you have per-cell TCR-contribution scores from a trained mvTCR model plus TCRdist similarities per specificity group, and you must validate that TCR contribution quantitatively tracks TCR sequence similarity across epitope groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-correlation-validation))
- **pearson-logistic-gsea-baseline-execution**: Use when a new TR–drug response inference method (e.g. RePhine) must be compared fairly against established mRNA- and target-based baselines on the same simulated noise or confounder scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pearson-logistic-gsea-baseline-execution))
- **pearson-neighborhood-similarity-scoring**: Use when you need to decide, cell by cell, whether a 2D embedding (t-SNE or UMAP) faithfully preserves pre-embedding neighborhood structure, or when optimizing hyperparameters like t-SNE perplexity by minimizing dubious embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-neighborhood-similarity-scoring))
- **pearson-r2-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a taxonomic classifier or compositional estimator against known mock/simulated community ground truth and you need quantitative correlation (Pearson r2) and error (L1) metrics on abundance profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/pearson-r2-accuracy-evaluation))
- **pearson-residual-computation-zinb**: Use when analyzing single-cell scRNA-seq count data (e.g. the pancreatic islet dataset with individual/sample IDs) to decide which genes require random intercepts before a full TWO-SIGMA mixed-model fit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pearson-residual-computation-zinb))
- **pearson-residual-matrix-computation**: Use when you have a raw scRNAseq count matrix and need the chi-squared residual matrix for CA dimension reduction, or when verifying a re-implementation of the corral_preproc count-to-residual pre-processing stage element-wise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pearson-residual-matrix-computation))
- **pearson-spearman-matrix-computation**: Use when a named-column catalog (e.g. galaxy semi-analytic mock data or SDSS supernova host properties) needs pairwise variable-similarity matrices to feed hierarchical clustering under different correlation choices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/pearson-spearman-matrix-computation))
- **pearsoncorrcoef**: Compute the PearsonCorrCoef metric — provided by torchmetrics. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute PearsonCorrCoef, or asks how to score with PearsonCorrCoef. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pearsoncorrcoef) · ★3)
- **pearsonr**: Compute the pearsonr metric — provided by scipy.stats. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute pearsonr, or asks how to score with pearsonr. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pearsonr) · ★3)
- **pearsonscontingencycoefficient**: Compute the PearsonsContingencyCoefficient metric — provided by torchmetrics. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute PearsonsContingencyCoefficient, or asks how to score with PearsonsContingencyCoefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pearsonscontingencycoefficient) · ★3)
- **Pearwrite**: Co-edit a hand-written HTML document with the user through Pearwrite, a local server that lets them type into the rendered page and comment on it in the browser while you edit the same file. Use when the user wants to edit or review an HTML document (report, handout, proposal) in the browser together with you, mentions Pearwrite, or sends a browser comment on an element that has a data-id attribute. ([jonasjancarik/pearwrite](https://github.com/jonasjancarik/pearwrite/tree/HEAD/skills/pearwrite))
- **peas**: Apply the PEAS framework (Performance measure, Environment, Actuators, Sensors) to specify or audit an AI agent’s task environment. Use when the user asks to create a PEAS list/table for a coding agent or workflow, translate a vague “how should the agent work?” into concrete success metrics and constraints, or add a PEAS section to AGENTS.md / PRD / design docs. ([taylorparsons/cortege-hackathon](https://github.com/taylorparsons/cortege-hackathon/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/peas) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **peas-capstone-report**: 逐步引導 Agent Studio 專題學生完成可繳交報告（專題介紹、左欄↔右欄互動、成果／創新／技術由學生主筆；Agent 代勞架構 Mermaid、全班相同 server 拓撲圖、MD／PPT／Word、Step 7 VCR 專題海報）。每則回覆前必讀 references/step-scripts.md 當前 step_id；Step 1–3 學生口述、對齊條列後才落檔；Step 6 三選一 PPT 風格 → 6b html2pptx → 6c docx；Step 7 必產 report/assets/專題海報.png。觸發：專題報告、capstone、Agent Studio 架構圖、server 配置圖、專題海報。 ([weeeen0602/apple](https://github.com/weeeen0602/apple/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/peas-capstone-report) · 2 copies)
- **peas-workshop-advanced-coach**: 當學生**已完成 WG-12～21**（單檔合併藍本）並做 **WG-22 拆檔**（`agent_core.py` + `main.py`）時使用。**新工作階段第一則**先 PEAS 品牌畫面與準備確認；確認後**先情境鋪陳**再進需求釐清。缺 **`prompts/`、`templates/`** 時自 `references/project_assets/` 複製至專案根。流程：Spec 對齊（2d′）→ 六欄契約 → **同對話 handoff 實作** → 驗收。起點 starter_main_wg21.py；標準 reference_agent_core.py + reference_main.py。觸發：peas-workshop-advanced-coach、PEAS workshop 進階教練、WG-22、拆檔教練、Agent.chat。 ([weeeen0602/apple](https://github.com/weeeen0602/apple/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/peas-workshop-advanced-coach) · 2 copies)
- **peasant-harvest-audit**: How to query the local peasant database for past harvest runs and the ingest/index audit log. Use whenever the user asks about harvest results, "what did the last harvest do", ingest or worker errors, why a session failed, was skipped, or was not re-indexed, run summaries, audit logs, per-session outcomes, diagnostics, reason codes, index history, or session origins — anything that needs `ingest_log`, `index_log`, `ingest_run_outcomes`, or the peasant SQLite store. - **peasants-quest-voice**: Write text-parser game responses in the voice and response behavior of Homestar Runner's Peasant's Quest (2004) — a snarky second-person narrator that mocks the player, escalates on repeated commands, and breaks the fourth wall. Domain-agnostic: apply the style to whatever world the current game is about (Microsoft Fabric, baseball, office life, sci-fi), always using that game's own nouns, characters, and commands. Use when authoring response tables, unknown-command fallbacks, repeat-command escalations, refusals, death/fail text, item descriptions, NPC lines, or hint text. - **peass-ng**: Operate PEASS-ng (Privilege Escalation Awesome Scripts SUITE) — the industry-standard post-exploitation enumeration toolkit covering LinPEAS and WinPEAS. - **peasy**: USE FOR: Implementing business rules as composable, testable rule objects using the Peasy framework. Use when building middle-tier validation pipelines for commands and services that require rule chaining, async validation, and conditional execution. It loads the Peasy Design System artifact as the source of truth and enforces its tokens, components, vo… ([KeyValueSoftwareSystems/peasy-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/KeyValueSoftwareSystems/peasy-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/peasy-design/skills/peasy-design))
- **peat**: ../../../skills/peat/SKILL.md ([flowerornament/peat](https://github.com/flowerornament/peat/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/peat) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **peat**: Use when a repository has a .peat/ directory or peat hooks, when setting peat up, or when a session is missing from the record. Finding the session and conversation behind a commit, change, bead or file before changing it; answering "why is it like this"; reading what a past session said and decided; searching prior work; time-travelling to what was known on a date. ([flowerornament/peat](https://github.com/flowerornament/peat/tree/HEAD/skills/peat) · ★1)
- **peated-catalog**: Catalogs or repairs Peated Bottle records directly for a named Bottle, brand, Series, distillery, bottler, or release set. Use for missing Bottles, wrong facts, Brand or Series decisions, images, aliases, references, duplicate review, or a dry-run inventory of such a set. - **peated-cli**: Use Peated's authenticated API client for OAuth-backed production API reads and writes through `pnpm cli auth` and `pnpm cli api`. Direct database-backed CLI command groups are legacy and unsupported for operations. ([dcramer/peated](https://github.com/dcramer/peated/tree/HEAD/skills/peated-cli) · ★101)
- **peated-qa**: Use when manually QAing Peated changes across the API, CLI, or web UI; selecting smoke checks after code changes; using agent-browser for Peated flows; verifying local behavior beyond tests and lint. - **peated-scraper-queue**: Moderates Peated retailer listings in the store-price match queue by hand, with evidence. - **peated-writing**: Writes and reviews user-facing Peated copy in the product's voice. Carries the exact geometry of the invitation card and its name-only variant, the A4 line sheet, the A4 monthly closing sheet, the rep ranking workbook's chrome, the measured pack inks (gold and aqua), the equal-optical-area pack law, the hexagon mark, the seeded honeycomb texture and the verifier. Use when building an invitation, a round-table or spea… ([sato-pharma/phoenix-people-workforce](https://github.com/sato-pharma/phoenix-people-workforce/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pedia-identity))

([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/physical-therapy/pediatric))
- **Pediatric Growth Reference Educator**: This skill explains the concepts behind pediatric growth references and standards: the WHO Child Growth Standards (0-5 years), the WHO 2007 growth reference (5-19 years), the difference between a standard and a reference, what z-scores and percentiles mean, and how growth charts are used in population research. It is educational only; it does not interpret any child's measurements, diagnose growth disorders, or advise care. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/skills/specialty-research/pediatric-growth-reference-educator) · ★5)
- **pediatric-assessment**: Use when asked to administer, score, or explain a Pediatric Assessment — child development screening covering growth, milestones, and immunization — grounded in FormExamples/form-examples. - **pediatric-audiology**: Pediatric Hearing Screening & Diagnostics: Auditory Brainstem Response (ABR) threshold testing, Distortion Product Otoacoustic Emissions (DPOAE), Visual Reinforcement Audiometry (VRA), and Conditioned Play Audiometry (CPA). ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/audiology/pediatric-audiology))
- **pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval**: Evaluates deep learning architectures for multi-class segmentation of pediatric brain tumors on MRI scans. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate tumor sub-regions (whole tumor, enhanced tumor, cystic component, edema) and assesses cross-domain generalizability to adult glioma data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PED BraTS 2024, CBTN, BraTS Adult Glioma 2023, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports lesion-wise Dice. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval**: This benchmark evaluates the ability of weakly supervised multiple instance learning models to classify pediatric brain tumors from whole-slide histopathology images. It probes fine-grained diagnostic discrimination across varying class granularities (2 to 7 classes) under conditions of class imbalance and limited data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Pediatric brain tumor WSI dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Macro F1. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-drug-dosing**: Weight-based and body-surface-area-based drug dosing calculator for pediatric patients. Includes common pediatric medications with age-appropriate dosing ranges. ([Open-Medica/open-medical-skills](https://github.com/Open-Medica/open-medical-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatric-drug-dosing) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **pediatric-emergency**: Pediatric emergency medicine — PALS approach, shock and sepsis, respiratory emergencies (asthma, croup, bronchiolitis, foreign body, epiglottitis), seizures and status epilepticus, head trauma (PECARN), dehydration, surgical abdomen in children, ingestions and button batteries, anaphylaxis, DKA, and child abuse recognition. Use for any acutely ill or injured child. ([sstarzh/medical-doctor](https://github.com/sstarzh/medical-doctor/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatric-emergency))
- **pediatric-fever-triage**: Use when a caregiver reports a child (age 0 to under 18) with fever or subjective warmth at night, with or without accompanying symptoms. Produces a three-way next-action decision — Call (emergency or hotline), Go (open pharmacy), or Wait (home self-care) — with the Yorukusu safety boundary on every output. Inputs include age, temperature (measured or subjective), fever duration, concurrent symptoms, antipyretic history, and the caller's Personal Health Context. Never outputs a diagnosis and never recommends prescription medication. ([Guttyo/yorukusu](https://github.com/Guttyo/yorukusu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pediatric-fever-triage) · ★1)
- **pediatric-growth-assessment**: Assesses pediatric growth using WHO and CDC growth charts with percentile and z-score calculations. Use when user asks to "plot growth", "check growth chart", "growth assessment", "growth percentiles", "is the child growing normally", "failure to thrive", "FTT workup", "pediatric BMI", mentions "growth velocity", "weight for age", "height for age", or needs growth trend analysis for a child. Do NOT use for adult BMI, adult weight management, or prenatal growth (use prenatal-visit-workflow). ([langcare/langcare-mcp-fhir](https://github.com/langcare/langcare-mcp-fhir/tree/HEAD/skills/core/specialty/pediatric-growth-assessment) · ★75)
- **pediatric-growth-charts**: Interpret pediatric growth measurements using CDC and WHO growth charts. Plots height, weight, BMI, and head circumference percentiles for age and gender. ([Open-Medica/open-medical-skills](https://github.com/Open-Medica/open-medical-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatric-growth-charts) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **pediatric-growth-reference-educator**: Explain the WHO Child Growth Standards and growth reference concepts for educational use. Educational and synthetic by design; outputs are drafts for review. - **pediatric-infectious-disease**: Common and dangerous pediatric infections — fever without a source by age, acute otitis media, strep pharyngitis, sinusitis, pneumonia, urinary tract infection, skin infections, Kawasaki disease and MIS-C, orbital cellulitis, meningitis, measles, pertussis, and antibiotic dosing/stewardship in children. Use whenever a child may have an infection. ([sstarzh/medical-doctor](https://github.com/sstarzh/medical-doctor/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatric-infectious-disease))
- **pediatric-metabolic-mma-expert**: 面向儿童遗传代谢病，尤其是甲基丙二酸血症（MMA）的医学专家技能。用于处理幼儿喂养、饮食结构、营养支持、代谢失衡风险、长期管理、国际指南比较、临床试验动态、不同国家/地区治疗实践差异、急性恶化识别、家属教育、随访框架，以及任何需要优先依据最新指南、共识、药品标签、专业学会和原始研究证据来判断的高风险医疗场景。 ([TheHtmler/LemonBabyDairyMini](https://github.com/TheHtmler/LemonBabyDairyMini/tree/HEAD/skills/pediatric-metabolic-mma-expert))
- **pediatric-obesity-consultation**: Guide a non-stigmatising pediatric obesity consultation using the 5As framework (Ask, Assess, Advise, Agree, Assist) and 4Ms assessment (Metabolic, Mechanical, Mental Health, Social Milieu), based on the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks how to approach a weight conversation with a child or family, how to use the 5As for obesity, how to assess a child with obesity, or how to conduct a non-judgmental obesity visit. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-consultation) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener**: Screen a child with obesity for red flags suggesting monogenic, syndromic, or secondary endocrine causes — distinguishing atypical from typical exogenous obesity. Trigger when a clinician asks whether obesity could be genetic or hormonal, suspects Prader-Willi syndrome, Cushing disease, hypothyroidism, ROHHAD, Bardet-Biedl syndrome, leptin deficiency, or craniopharyngioma, or when a child has early-onset severe obesity, short stature with obesity, hyperphagia, hypotonia, dysmorphic features, or developmental delay with weight gain. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector**: Select the right treatment tier for a child with obesity — behavioural, pharmacologic, or surgical — using the CMAJ 2025 pediatric obesity guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks what to do for a child with obesity, which intervention to start, whether to escalate treatment, or when to consider medication or surgery for pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector**: Select the right pharmacologic agent for a child aged 12+ with obesity using the CMAJ 2025 guideline — choosing between GLP-1 receptor agonists, metformin, or orlistat with monitoring guidance. Trigger when a clinician asks which medication to use for pediatric obesity, whether to start semaglutide or metformin in a child, or how to manage obesity pharmacologically in adolescents. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener**: Screen a child aged 13+ with obesity for eligibility for bariatric surgery (laparoscopic sleeve gastrectomy or Roux-en-Y gastric bypass) using the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks whether a child qualifies for weight loss surgery, bariatric surgery in adolescents, sleeve gastrectomy in a teenager, or when to refer for surgical management of pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)

- **pediatric-surgeon**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a pediatric general surgeon — triaging bilious vomiting or an acute abdomen in a neonate, deciding operative timing and staged-versus-definitive repair for congenital anomalies (malrotation, gastroschisis, CDH, biliary atresia), managing necrotizing enterocolitis by clinical stage, or working an acute scrotum against a salvage-time clock. - **pedido-insumos**: Use when pending client inputs need to be chased by e-mail in a project that keeps deltaspec discovery records (docs/discovery/questions/, pedido-validacao-*.md, debts/) — produces one ready-to-paste e-mail per owner, ranked and with an explicit deadline. Triggers include "/deltaspec:pedido-insumos", "cobrar os insumos", "gerar e-mail de cobrança", "quem está devendo insumo", "pedir os dados que faltam", "cobrar o cliente". Not for the reconciliation of an input that already arrived — that is rodada-insumos. ([iuripereira/deltaspec](https://github.com/iuripereira/deltaspec/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-insumos) · ★3)

Não envia ao hospital (isso é a /prontuario). Use quando o operador pedir "/pedido-prontuario 2342", "prepara o pedido de prontuário do 2342", "pedido hospitalar do fulano". - **pedido-registro-inpi**: Monta o deposito do pedido de registro de marca no e-Marcas — define natureza e forma de apresentacao (nominativa/mista/figurativa/tridimensional/de posicao), a especificacao de produtos/servicos por UMA classe NCL, e a GRU correta (389 pre-aprovada ou 394 livre, por classe, paga ANTES de peticionar). Checa as proibicoes do art. 124 LPI antes de enviar. Use depois do contrato assinado e das GRUs, ou quando o operador disser "fazer o deposito", "registrar a marca no INPI", "monta o pedido no e-Marcas", "depositar a marca", "preencher o e-Marcas", "/deposito-marca". - **pedido-tutela-urgencia**: PEDIDO-TUTELA-URGENCIA — Estrutura pedido de tutela de urgencia (CPC art. 300) acessorio a inicial de execucao, monitoria ou cobranca. Cabe arresto (art. 830), indisponibilidade de bens via SISBAJUD (art. 854), bloqueio de veiculos (RENAJUD), protesto extrajudicial preventivo. Exige FUMUS BONI IURIS + PERICULUM IN MORA. Use quando ha risco de dilapidacao patrimonial ou de prescricao iminente. ([sbroggioadv/execucao-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/execucao-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/execucao-adv-os/skills/pedido-tutela-urgencia) · ★1)
- **pedido-unico**: Conferente de pedido único antes do executor e de permanência da lacuna citada na tentativa seguinte. ([ortizpedroso/nextcode](https://github.com/ortizpedroso/nextcode/tree/HEAD/skills/pedido-unico))
- **pedido-uniformizacao**: Elabora pedido de uniformização ao Conselho Pleno do CRSS quando há divergência entre Câmaras sobre tese previdenciária. Skill técnica específica — exige demonstração da divergência + tese a uniformizar. Pode ser usado em paralelo a recurso à Câmara (preliminar de uniformização). Use sempre que o advogado disser: "uniformização", "divergência entre câmaras", "conselho pleno", "tese uniforme", "pedido uniformização CRSS", "afetação ao pleno". Não use pra recurso comum (use [[recurso-camara-julgamento]]). ([raphaelRPC/dr-claudio](https://github.com/raphaelRPC/dr-claudio/tree/HEAD/familias/dr-claudio/dr-claudio-previdenciario-administrativo/skills/pedido-uniformizacao) · ★11)

- **pedido_guiado**: Al iniciar «quiero hacer un pedido», consulta Eventos cercanos para la sede activa. Presenta fuentes y límites; un fallo no significa que no haya eventos y permite reintentar explícitamente o continuar sin repetir búsquedas automáticamente. ([pieromontalvof-ux/PROGRAMACION-3](https://github.com/pieromontalvof-ux/PROGRAMACION-3/tree/HEAD/backend/app/multiagente/api/skills/pedido_guiado))

- **pedidos**: Ver pedidos, cotizar el envío, confirmar contraentregas de riesgo, generar o cancelar la guía y resolver lo que el agente dejó esperando aprobación. Úsala si dicen "qué pedidos hay", "envía este pedido", "cotiza", "genera la guía", "cancela la guía" o "qué tengo pendiente". - **pedigree-kinship-matrix-preparation**: Use when RNA-seq samples come from related individuals (family/pedigree cohort) and you want to model pairwise genetic relatedness as a covariance random effect, or to compare kinship vs non-kinship model fit via per-gene AIC. - **pedigree-sv-genotype-concordance-evaluation**: Use when you have HiFi WGS read sets for multiple related samples (e.g. a pedigree like CEPH-1463) and need to compare SV callers by genotype concordance with known inheritance rather than a truth-set benchmark. - **pedigree-trio-genotype-analysis**: Use when a parent–parent–child trio of haplotype-resolved long-read alignments is available and you need to flag each genotyped tandem repeat as Mendelian consistent or inconsistent, as done for the HG00731/HG00732/HG00733 Puerto Rican PB trio. - **peec-checkup**: Read-only health check for an existing Peec AI project. - **peec-cluster**: Turns a Peec AI prompt set into strategic content zones — not keyword groups. Groups prompts by buyer intent + funnel stage + visibility gap + shared demand signals, producing 4–8 "content zones" with the competitive weakness to attack, supporting evidence (forum quotes, SERP patterns), and a rank-ordered next-move per zone. Use when a Peec project has 20+ prompts and needs a topic architecture — not a flat content calendar. Output is a strategic map, not a list. ([AntonioBlago/peec-ai-skills](https://github.com/AntonioBlago/peec-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/peec-cluster) · ★10)

- **peec-content-intel**: Content-intelligence workflow that turns a Peec AI visibility gap into a publish-ready content brief. Uses Query Fan-Out to expand one prompt into 5–8 sub-queries and scores competitor URLs for attackability. Use when the user wants to find content opportunities, evaluate competitor content, build a content brief from Peec data, or discover what content wins specific AI prompts. - **peec-gap-read**: Canonical recipe for reading a brand's gap data from the Peec AI MCP for a specific article. - **peec-learn**: Cross-project pattern layer for the Peec AI growth loop. After any Peec skill completes (or after peec-report closes a cycle), extract 1–3 concrete patterns from the output and persist them to SkillMind via mcp__skillmind__add_pattern / remember. On the next orchestrator run, recall matching patterns and pass them in as priors — so lessons learned on project A inform decisions on project B.
